/**
 * 採購收貨過帳（Bundle）
 * - 一次完成：goods_receipt + goods_receipt_item + lot + inventory_movement(IN) + 回寫 purchase_order_item.received_qty + purchase_order.status
 *
 * params:
 * - gr_id, po_id, receipt_date, warehouse, remark(optional), created_by, created_at(optional)
 * - lines_json: JSON array [{ po_item_id, received_qty, unit, manufacture_date?, expiry_date? }]
 */
function postGoodsReceiptBundle(p){
  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  lock.waitLock(20000);
  try{
    const grId = String(p && p.gr_id || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    const poId = String(p && p.po_id || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    const receiptDate = String(p && p.receipt_date || "").trim();
    const warehouse = String(p && p.warehouse || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    const remark = String(p && p.remark || "");
    const actor = String(p && (p.created_by || p.updated_by) || "").trim();
    if(!grId) return jsonError("gr_id required");
    if(!poId) return jsonError("po_id required");
    if(!receiptDate) return jsonError("receipt_date required");
    if(!warehouse) return jsonError("warehouse required");
    if(!actor) return jsonError("created_by required");
    const perm = requireErpRole_(actor, ["ADMIN","OP","WH","WAREHOUSE"]);
    if (perm) return perm;
    const expectedItems = Number(p.expected_existed_goods_receipt_item_count || 0);
    if(isNaN(expectedItems)) return jsonError("expected_existed_goods_receipt_item_count invalid");

    // 防重：收貨單已存在則拒絕（避免重送造成重複入庫）
    const existed = (function(){
      const sheet = getSheet("goods_receipt");
      if(!sheet) return null;
      const data = sheet.getDataRange().getDisplayValues();
      if(data.length < 2) return null;
      const headers = data[0];
      const idx = headers.indexOf("gr_id");
      if(idx === -1) return null;
      for(let i=1;i<data.length;i++){
        if(String(data[i][idx] || "").trim().toUpperCase() === grId) return rowToObject(headers, data[i]);
      }
      return null;
    })();
    if(existed){
      const st = String(existed.status || "").trim().toUpperCase();
      if(st === "POSTED") return jsonError("Goods receipt already POSTED");
      if(st === "CANCELLED") return jsonError("Goods receipt already CANCELLED");
      return jsonError("Goods receipt already exists");
    }
    const existedItemCount = (function(){
      const sheet = getSheet("goods_receipt_item");
      if(!sheet) return 0;
      const data = sheet.getDataRange().getDisplayValues();
      if(data.length < 2) return 0;
      const headers = data[0];
      const idxGid = headers.indexOf("gr_id");
      if(idxGid === -1) return 0;
      let c = 0;
      for(let i=1;i<data.length;i++){
        if(String(data[i][idxGid] || "").trim().toUpperCase() === grId) c++;
      }
      return c;
    })();
    if(existedItemCount !== expectedItems){
      return jsonError("Goods receipt items changed. Please reload and try again");
    }

    const po = (function(){
      const sheet = getSheet("purchase_order");
      if(!sheet) return null;
      const data = sheet.getDataRange().getDisplayValues();
      if(data.length < 2) return null;
      const headers = data[0];
      const idx = headers.indexOf("po_id");
      if(idx === -1) return null;
      for(let i=1;i<data.length;i++){
        if(String(data[i][idx] || "").trim().toUpperCase() === poId) return rowToObject(headers, data[i]);
      }
      return null;
    })();
    if(!po) return jsonError("PO not found: " + poId);
    if(String(po.status || "").trim().toUpperCase() === "CANCELLED") return jsonError("PO is CANCELLED");

    let lines = [];
    try{ lines = JSON.parse(String(p.lines_json || "[]")); }catch(_e){ return jsonError("lines_json must be valid JSON array"); }
    if(!Array.isArray(lines) || lines.length === 0) return jsonError("lines_json required");
    var expectedReceivedByPoItem = {};
    try{
      expectedReceivedByPoItem = JSON.parse(String(p.expected_received_by_po_item_json || "{}"));
      if(!expectedReceivedByPoItem || typeof expectedReceivedByPoItem !== "object" || Array.isArray(expectedReceivedByPoItem)){
        return jsonError("expected_received_by_po_item_json invalid");
      }
    }catch(_eExpected2){
      return jsonError("expected_received_by_po_item_json invalid");
    }

    // transaction_id：每次收貨過帳生成一條交易串號（TX-時間戳）
    const txId = buildTxId_();

    const map = (function(){
      const sheet = getSheet("purchase_order_item");
      if(!sheet) return {};
      const data = sheet.getDataRange().getDisplayValues();
      if(data.length < 2) return {};
      const headers = data[0];
      const idxPo = headers.indexOf("po_id");
      if(idxPo === -1) return {};
      const out = {};
      for(let i=1;i<data.length;i++){
        if(String(data[i][idxPo] || "").trim().toUpperCase() !== poId) continue;
        const o = rowToObject(headers, data[i]);
        const k = String(o.po_item_id || "").trim();
        if(k) out[k] = o;
      }
      return out;
    })();

    // 來源級防重（雙分頁）：比對每筆 po_item 已收數量快照（CAS）
    const actualReceivedByPoItem = {};
    Object.keys(map || {}).forEach(function(k){
      const row = map[k] || {};
      actualReceivedByPoItem[k] = Number(row.received_qty || 0);
    });

    // 先完整驗證（避免驗證失敗時先建立主檔，留下 0 明細單）
    for(let i=0;i<lines.length;i++){
      const ln = lines[i] || {};
      const poItemId = String(ln.po_item_id || "").trim();
      const qty = Number(ln.received_qty || 0);
      if(!poItemId) return jsonError("po_item_id required (lines[" + i + "])");
      if(!(qty > 0)) return jsonError("received_qty must be > 0 (lines[" + i + "])");
      const item = map[poItemId];
      if(!item) return jsonError("PO item not found: " + poItemId);
      const expectedReceived = Number(expectedReceivedByPoItem[poItemId] != null ? expectedReceivedByPoItem[poItemId] : 0);
      if(isNaN(expectedReceived)) return jsonError("expected_received_by_po_item_json invalid value: " + poItemId);
      const actualReceived = Number(actualReceivedByPoItem[poItemId] || 0);
      if(Math.abs(actualReceived - expectedReceived) > 1e-9){
        return jsonErrorCode("ERR_SOURCE_CHANGED", "PO source changed. Please reload and try again");
      }
      const ordered = Number(item.order_qty || 0);
      const received = Number(item.received_qty || 0);
      const remain = Math.max(0, ordered - received);
      if(qty > remain) return jsonError("received_qty exceeds remaining: " + poItemId);
      const unit = String(ln.unit || item.unit || "").trim();
      if(!unit) return jsonError("unit required (lines[" + i + "])");
      const mfg = String(ln.manufacture_date || "").trim();
      const exp = String(ln.expiry_date || "").trim();
      if(mfg && exp && exp < mfg) return jsonError("expiry_date cannot be earlier than manufacture_date (lines[" + i + "])");
    }

    // 全部驗證通過後才進入 idempotency gate（避免重送重寫）
    const idem = consumeIdempotencyKey_("POST_GOODS_RECEIPT_BUNDLE", p && p.idempotency_key, 600);
    if(!idem.ok) return jsonErrorCode("ERR_DUPLICATE_REQUEST", "Duplicate request detected. Please reload and try again");

    // 全部驗證通過後才建立收貨單（固定 POSTED）
    var _failGrHdr = bundleStepFail_(createGeneric("goods_receipt", {
      _system_write: "1",
      gr_id: grId,
      po_id: poId,
      transaction_id: txId,
      parent_ref_type: "PO",
      parent_ref_id: poId,
      receipt_date: receiptDate,
      warehouse: warehouse,
      status: "POSTED",
      remark: remark,
      created_by: actor,
      created_at: p.created_at || nowIso16(),
      updated_by: "",
      updated_at: ""
    }, "gr_id"));
    if(_failGrHdr) return _failGrHdr;

    let created = 0;
    for(let i=0;i<lines.length;i++){
      const ln = lines[i] || {};
      const poItemId = String(ln.po_item_id || "").trim();
      const qty = Number(ln.received_qty || 0);
      if(!poItemId) return jsonError("po_item_id required (lines[" + i + "])");
      if(!(qty > 0)) return jsonError("received_qty must be > 0 (lines[" + i + "])");
      const item = map[poItemId];
      if(!item) return jsonError("PO item not found: " + poItemId);
      const expectedReceived = Number(expectedReceivedByPoItem[poItemId] != null ? expectedReceivedByPoItem[poItemId] : 0);
      if(isNaN(expectedReceived)) return jsonError("expected_received_by_po_item_json invalid value: " + poItemId);
      const actualReceived = Number(actualReceivedByPoItem[poItemId] || 0);
      if(Math.abs(actualReceived - expectedReceived) > 1e-9){
        return jsonError("PO source changed. Please reload and try again");
      }
      const ordered = Number(item.order_qty || 0);
      const received = Number(item.received_qty || 0);
      const remain = Math.max(0, ordered - received);
      if(qty > remain) return jsonError("received_qty exceeds remaining: " + poItemId);
      const productId = String(item.product_id || "").trim();
      const unit = String(ln.unit || item.unit || "").trim();
      if(!unit) return jsonError("unit required (lines[" + i + "])");

      const lotId = "LOT-" + new Date().getTime() + "-" + i;
      const mfg = String(ln.manufacture_date || "").trim();
      const exp = String(ln.expiry_date || "").trim();
      if(mfg && exp && exp < mfg) return jsonError("expiry_date cannot be earlier than manufacture_date (lines[" + i + "])");

      // lot
      var _failGrLot = bundleStepFail_(createGeneric("lot", {
        lot_id: lotId,
        product_id: productId,
        warehouse_id: warehouse || "MAIN",
        source_type: "PURCHASE",
        source_id: grId,
        qty: String(qty),
        unit: unit,
        type: "",
        status: "PENDING",
        inventory_status: "ACTIVE",
        received_date: receiptDate,
        manufacture_date: mfg,
        expiry_date: exp,
        remark: "",
        created_by: actor,
        created_at: nowIso16(),
        updated_by: "",
        updated_at: "",
        system_remark: "PO:" + poId + " / ITEM:" + poItemId
      }, "lot_id"));
      if(_failGrLot) return _failGrLot;

      // movement IN
      const mvRes = createInventoryMovementUnlocked_({
        movement_id: "MV-" + new Date().getTime() + "-" + i,
        movement_type: "IN",
        lot_id: lotId,
        product_id: productId,
        warehouse_id: warehouse || "MAIN",
        transaction_id: txId,
        parent_ref_type: "GOODS_RECEIPT",
        parent_ref_id: grId,
        qty: String(Math.abs(qty)),
        unit: unit,
        ref_type: "GOODS_RECEIPT",
        ref_id: grId,
        issued_to: "",
        remark: "",
        created_by: actor,
        created_at: nowIso16(),
        updated_by: "",
        updated_at: "",
        system_remark: "PO IN: " + poId
      });
      var _failGrMv = bundleStepFail_(mvRes);
      if(_failGrMv) return _failGrMv;

      // receipt item
      var _failGrItem = bundleStepFail_(createGeneric("goods_receipt_item", {
        _system_write: "1",
        gr_item_id: "GRI-" + grId + "-" + String(created + 1).padStart(3, "0"),
        gr_id: grId,
        po_id: poId,
        po_item_id: poItemId,
        product_id: productId,
        transaction_id: txId,
        parent_ref_type: "GOODS_RECEIPT",
        parent_ref_id: grId,
        received_qty: String(qty),
        unit: unit,
        lot_id: lotId,
        remark: "",
        created_by: actor,
        created_at: nowIso16(),
        updated_by: "",
        updated_at: ""
      }, "gr_item_id"));
      if(_failGrItem) return _failGrItem;

      // 回寫 PO item received_qty
      var _failGrPoi = bundleStepFail_(updateGeneric("purchase_order_item", {
        po_item_id: poItemId,
        received_qty: String(received + qty),
        updated_by: actor,
        updated_at: nowIso16()
      }, "po_item_id"));
      if(_failGrPoi) return _failGrPoi;
      created++;
    }

    // PO 狀態：依明細收貨量判斷 OPEN / PARTIAL / CLOSED（允許分批收貨）
    if (created > 0) {
      const nextPoStatus = calcPurchaseOrderStatusByItems_(poId);
      var _failGrPo = bundleStepFail_(updateGeneric("purchase_order", {
        po_id: poId,
        status: nextPoStatus,
        updated_by: actor,
        updated_at: nowIso16()
      }, "po_id"));
      if(_failGrPo) return _failGrPo;
    }

    writeAuditBundle_("goods_receipt", grId, "POST_GOODS_RECEIPT", { gr_id: grId, created_lots: created }, actor);
    return jsonSuccess({ message: "POSTED", gr_id: grId, created_lots: created });
  }finally{
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

/**
 * 作廢採購收貨（Bundle）
 * - 一次完成：ADJUST 沖銷原 IN、Lot→VOID／QA REJECTED、goods_receipt→CANCELLED、回退 purchase_order_item.received_qty + 回寫 purchase_order.status
 *
 * params:
 * - gr_id
 * - void_reason_code (optional)
 * - void_reason_label (optional)
 * - void_reason_note (optional)
 * - updated_by
 */
function cancelGoodsReceiptBundle(p){
  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  lock.waitLock(20000);
  try{
    const grId = String(p && p.gr_id || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    if(!grId) return jsonError("gr_id required");
    const actor = String(p && (p.updated_by || p.created_by) || "").trim();
    if(!actor) return jsonError("updated_by required");
    const perm = requireErpRole_(actor, ["ADMIN","OP","WH","WAREHOUSE"]);
    if (perm) return perm;

    const gr = getOne("goods_receipt", "gr_id", grId);
    if(!gr) return jsonError("Goods receipt not found: " + grId);
    const st = String(gr.status || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    if(st === "CANCELLED") return jsonError("Goods receipt already CANCELLED");
    const txId = normalizeTxId_(gr.transaction_id) || buildTxId_();

    const itemsSheet = getSheet("goods_receipt_item");
    if(!itemsSheet) return jsonError("goods_receipt_item sheet not found");
    const itemsData = itemsSheet.getDataRange().getDisplayValues();
    if(itemsData.length < 2) return jsonError("No goods receipt items");
    const itemsHeaders = itemsData[0];
    const idxGid = itemsHeaders.indexOf("gr_id");
    if(idxGid === -1) return jsonError("gr_id column not found in goods_receipt_item");
    const items = [];
    for(let i=1;i<itemsData.length;i++){
      if(String(itemsData[i][idxGid] || "").trim().toUpperCase() === grId){
        items.push(rowToObject(itemsHeaders, itemsData[i]));
      }
    }
    if(items.length === 0) return jsonError("Goods receipt items not found");

    const mvSheet = getSheet("inventory_movement");
    if(!mvSheet) return jsonError("inventory_movement sheet not found");
    // 防重：已有 CANCEL movement
    const mvData = mvSheet.getDataRange().getDisplayValues();
    if(mvData.length >= 2){
      const mvHeaders = mvData[0];
      const rtIdx = mvHeaders.indexOf("ref_type");
      const ridx = mvHeaders.indexOf("ref_id");
      if(rtIdx !== -1 && ridx !== -1){
        for(let i=1;i<mvData.length;i++){
          if(String(mvData[i][rtIdx] || "").toUpperCase() === "GOODS_RECEIPT_CANCEL" &&
             String(mvData[i][ridx] || "") === grId){
            return jsonError("Goods receipt already has cancel reversal movements");
          }
        }
      }
    }

    const code = String(p && p.void_reason_code || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    const label = String(p && p.void_reason_label || "").trim();
    const note = String(p && p.void_reason_note || "").trim();
    const voidLine = (function(){
      if(!code && !label && !note) return "";
      let s = "原因：" + (label || code || "");
      if(note) s += " / " + note;
      return s;
    })();
    const adjRemark = voidLine || "作廢沖銷";
    const voidTag = code ? (" | VOID:" + code) : "";

    const plan = [];
    for(let i=0;i<items.length;i++){
      const it = items[i] || {};
      const lotId = String(it.lot_id || "").trim();
      if(!lotId) return jsonError("lot_id missing in goods_receipt_item");

      const inMv = (function(){
        const data = mvSheet.getDataRange().getDisplayValues();
        if(data.length < 2) return null;
        const headers = data[0];
        const idxLot = headers.indexOf("lot_id");
        const idxType = headers.indexOf("movement_type");
        const idxRefT = headers.indexOf("ref_type");
        const idxRefI = headers.indexOf("ref_id");
        if(idxLot===-1||idxType===-1||idxRefT===-1||idxRefI===-1) return null;
        for(let r=1;r<data.length;r++){
          if(String(data[r][idxLot]||"") === lotId &&
             String(data[r][idxType]||"").toUpperCase() === "IN" &&
             String(data[r][idxRefT]||"").toUpperCase() === "GOODS_RECEIPT" &&
             String(data[r][idxRefI]||"") === grId){
            return rowToObject(headers, data[r]);
          }
        }
        return null;
      })();
      if(!inMv) return jsonError("IN movement not found for lot " + lotId);
      const inQty = Math.abs(Number(inMv.qty || 0));
      const failGrCancelAvail = assertLotMovementQty_(lotId, inQty, "Cancel goods receipt");
      if(failGrCancelAvail) return failGrCancelAvail;
      plan.push({ it, inMv, lotId, inQty });
    }

    // 全部驗證通過後才進入 idempotency gate（避免重送重寫）
    const idem = consumeIdempotencyKey_("CANCEL_GOODS_RECEIPT_BUNDLE", p && p.idempotency_key, 600);
    if(!idem.ok) return jsonError("Duplicate request detected. Please reload and try again");

    for(let i=0;i<plan.length;i++){
      const x = plan[i];
      const inMv = x.inMv;
      var _failCgrMv = bundleStepFail_(createInventoryMovementUnlocked_({
        movement_id: buildId_("MV"),
        movement_type: "ADJUST",
        lot_id: x.lotId,
        product_id: String(inMv.product_id || ""),
        warehouse_id: String(gr.warehouse || inMv.warehouse_id || "MAIN").trim().toUpperCase() || "MAIN",
        transaction_id: txId,
        parent_ref_type: "GOODS_RECEIPT",
        parent_ref_id: grId,
        qty: String(-Math.abs(x.inQty)),
        unit: String(inMv.unit || ""),
        ref_type: "GOODS_RECEIPT_CANCEL",
        ref_id: grId,
        issued_to: "",
        remark: adjRemark,
        created_by: actor,
        created_at: nowIso16(),
        updated_by: "",
        updated_at: "",
        system_remark: "REVERSAL(IN) of " + String(inMv.movement_id || "") + voidTag
      }));
      if(_failCgrMv) return _failCgrMv;
    }

    for(let i=0;i<plan.length;i++){
      var _failCgrLot = bundleStepFail_(updateGeneric("lot", {
        lot_id: plan[i].lotId,
        inventory_status: "VOID",
        status: "REJECTED",
        updated_by: actor,
        updated_at: nowIso16()
      }, "lot_id"));
      if(_failCgrLot) return _failCgrLot;
    }

    // 回退 PO item 已收
    for(let i=0;i<items.length;i++){
      const it = items[i] || {};
      const poItemId = String(it.po_item_id || "").trim();
      if(!poItemId) continue;
      const poi = getOne("purchase_order_item", "po_item_id", poItemId);
      if(!poi) continue;
      const dec = Number(it.received_qty || 0);
      const next = Math.max(0, Number(poi.received_qty || 0) - dec);
      var _failCgrPoi = bundleStepFail_(updateGeneric("purchase_order_item", {
        po_item_id: poItemId,
        received_qty: String(next),
        updated_by: actor,
        updated_at: nowIso16()
      }, "po_item_id"));
      if(_failCgrPoi) return _failCgrPoi;
    }

    // goods_receipt status + remark
    const prevRemark = String(gr.remark || "").trim();
    const nextRemark = voidLine ? (prevRemark ? (prevRemark + "\n[作廢 " + nowIso16() + "] " + voidLine) : ("[作廢 " + nowIso16() + "] " + voidLine)) : prevRemark;
    var _failCgrHdr = bundleStepFail_(updateGeneric("goods_receipt", {
      gr_id: grId,
      transaction_id: txId,
      parent_ref_type: String(gr.po_id || "") ? "PO" : "",
      parent_ref_id: String(gr.po_id || "").trim().toUpperCase(),
      status: "CANCELLED",
      remark: nextRemark,
      updated_by: actor,
      updated_at: nowIso16(),
      _system_write: "1"
    }, "gr_id"));
    if(_failCgrHdr) return _failCgrHdr;

    // PO status：若仍有未作廢收貨單 → CLOSED；否則 OPEN（除非 PO 已取消）
    try{
      const poId = String(gr.po_id || "").trim().toUpperCase();
      const po = getOne("purchase_order", "po_id", poId);
      if(po && String(po.status || "").toUpperCase() !== "CANCELLED"){
        const sheet = getSheet("goods_receipt");
        const data = sheet.getDataRange().getDisplayValues();
        const headers = data[0];
        const idxPo = headers.indexOf("po_id");
        const idxSt = headers.indexOf("status");
        let hasActive = false;
        for(let i=1;i<data.length;i++){
          if(String(data[i][idxPo]||"").trim().toUpperCase() !== poId) continue;
          if(String(data[i][idxSt]||"").trim().toUpperCase() !== "CANCELLED"){
            hasActive = true; break;
          }
        }
        // 作廢收貨後：依明細收貨量判斷 OPEN / PARTIAL / CLOSED（不以是否存在收貨單判斷）
        const nextPoStatus = calcPurchaseOrderStatusByItems_(poId);
        var _failCgrPo = bundleStepFail_(updateGeneric("purchase_order", {
          po_id: poId,
          status: nextPoStatus,
          updated_by: actor,
          updated_at: nowIso16()
        }, "po_id"));
        if(_failCgrPo) return _failCgrPo;
      }
    }catch(_e){}

    writeAuditBundle_("goods_receipt", grId, "CANCEL_GOODS_RECEIPT", { gr_id: grId }, actor);
    return jsonSuccess({ message: "CANCELLED", gr_id: grId });
  }finally{
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

function updateGoodsReceiptItem(p) {
  return jsonError("Direct update_goods_receipt_item is not allowed");
}

function deleteGoodsReceiptItem(p) {
  return jsonError("Deletion is not allowed. Use cancel receipt flow instead");
}

/* =================================================
   PROCESS ORDER（委外加工）
================================================= */

function listProcessOrder() {
  return listGeneric("process_order");
}

function createProcessOrder(p) {
  return jsonError("Direct create_process_order is not allowed. Use create_process_order_cmd instead");
}

function updateProcessOrder(p) {
  return jsonError("Direct update_process_order is not allowed. Use update_process_order_header_cmd or bundles instead");
}

function deleteProcessOrder(p) {
  return jsonError("Deletion is not allowed. Use cancel process order flow instead");
}

function listProcessOrderInput() {
  return listGeneric("process_order_input");
}

function listProcessOrderInputByOrder(p) {
  const id = String(p && p.process_order_id || "").trim();
  if (!id) return jsonError("process_order_id required");
  const sheet = getSheet("process_order_input");
  if (!sheet) return jsonSuccess({ data: [] });
  const data = sheet.getDataRange().getDisplayValues();
  if (data.length < 2) return jsonSuccess({ data: [] });
  const headers = data[0];
  const idx = headers.indexOf("process_order_id");
  if (idx === -1) return jsonError("process_order_id column not found");
  const out = [];
  for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (String(data[i][idx] || "") === id) out.push(rowToObject(headers, data[i]));
  }
  return jsonSuccess({ data: out });
}

function createProcessOrderInput(p) {
  return jsonError("Direct create_process_order_input is not allowed. Use issue_process_order_bundle instead");
}

function updateProcessOrderInput(p) {
  return jsonError("Direct update_process_order_input is not allowed. Use update_process_order_input_remark instead");
}

function deleteProcessOrderInput(p) {
  return jsonError("Deletion is not allowed. Use retract/void flow instead");
}

function listProcessOrderOutput() {
  return listGeneric("process_order_output");
}

function listProcessOrderOutputByOrder(p) {
  const id = String(p && p.process_order_id || "").trim();
  if (!id) return jsonError("process_order_id required");
  const sheet = getSheet("process_order_output");
  if (!sheet) return jsonSuccess({ data: [] });
  const data = sheet.getDataRange().getDisplayValues();
  if (data.length < 2) return jsonSuccess({ data: [] });
  const headers = data[0];
  const idx = headers.indexOf("process_order_id");
  if (idx === -1) return jsonError("process_order_id column not found");
  const out = [];
  for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (String(data[i][idx] || "") === id) out.push(rowToObject(headers, data[i]));
  }
  return jsonSuccess({ data: out });
}

function createProcessOrderOutput(p) {
  return jsonError("Direct create_process_order_output is not allowed. Use receive_process_output_bundle instead");
}

function updateProcessOrderOutput(p) {
  return jsonError("Direct update_process_order_output is not allowed. Use update_process_order_output_remark instead");
}

function deleteProcessOrderOutput(p) {
  return jsonError("Deletion is not allowed. Use void output flow instead");
}

/**
 * 建立加工單（Command）
 * - 避免 direct create_process_order 造成繞過規則
 * params: process_order_id, process_type, source_type(optional), supplier_id, planned_date(optional), remark(optional), created_by
 */
function createProcessOrderCmd(p){
  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  lock.waitLock(20000);
  try{
    const id = String(p && p.process_order_id || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    if(!id) return jsonError("process_order_id required");
    const actor = String(p && (p.created_by || p.updated_by) || "").trim();
    if(!actor) return jsonError("created_by required");
    const perm = requireErpRole_(actor, ["ADMIN","OP","WH","WAREHOUSE"]);
    if (perm) return perm;

    const existed = getOne("process_order","process_order_id",id);
    if(existed) return jsonError("Process order already exists");

    const processType = String(p && p.process_type || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    if(!processType) return jsonError("process_type required");
    const supplierId = String(p && p.supplier_id || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    if(!supplierId) return jsonError("supplier_id required");
    const sourceType = String(p && p.source_type || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    const plannedDate = String(p && p.planned_date || "").trim();
    const remark = String(p && p.remark || "");

    const txId = normalizeTxId_(p && p.transaction_id) || buildTxId_();
    const parentRefType = String(p && p.parent_ref_type || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    const parentRefId = String(p && p.parent_ref_id || "").trim().toUpperCase();

    var _failProcCreate = bundleStepFail_(createGeneric("process_order", {
      process_order_id: id,
      process_type: processType,
      source_type: sourceType,
      supplier_id: supplierId,
      transaction_id: txId,
      parent_ref_type: parentRefType,
      parent_ref_id: parentRefId,
      planned_date: plannedDate,
      status: "OPEN",
      remark: remark,
      created_by: actor,
      created_at: p.created_at || nowIso16(),
      updated_by: "",
      updated_at: ""
    }, "process_order_id"));
    if (_failProcCreate) return _failProcCreate;
    return jsonSuccess({ message:"Created", process_order_id: id });
  }finally{
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

/**
 * 更新加工單主檔（Command）
 * - 只允許更新 remark / planned_date（避免 direct update_process_order）
 * params: process_order_id, planned_date(optional), remark(optional), updated_by
 */
function updateProcessOrderHeaderCmd(p){
  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  lock.waitLock(20000);
  try{
    const id = String(p && p.process_order_id || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    if(!id) return jsonError("process_order_id required");
    const actor = String(p && (p.updated_by || p.created_by) || "").trim();
    if(!actor) return jsonError("updated_by required");
    const perm = requireErpRole_(actor, ["ADMIN","OP","WH","WAREHOUSE"]);
    if (perm) return perm;

    const po = getOne("process_order","process_order_id",id);
    if(!po) return jsonError("Process order not found");
    const st = String(po.status || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    if(st === "CANCELLED") return jsonError("Process order is CANCELLED");
    if(st === "POSTED") return jsonError("Process order already POSTED");

    var _failHdr = bundleStepFail_(updateGeneric("process_order", {
      process_order_id: id,
      planned_date: String(p && p.planned_date || ""),
      remark: String(p && p.remark || ""),
      updated_by: actor,
      updated_at: p.updated_at || nowIso16()
    }, "process_order_id"));
    if (_failHdr) return _failHdr;
    return jsonSuccess({ message:"Updated", process_order_id: id });
  }finally{
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

/**
 * 更新投料備註（Command）
 * params: process_input_id, remark, updated_by
 */
function updateProcessOrderInputRemark(p){
  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  lock.waitLock(20000);
  try{
    const id = String(p && p.process_input_id || "").trim();
    if(!id) return jsonError("process_input_id required");
    const actor = String(p && (p.updated_by || p.created_by) || "").trim();
    if(!actor) return jsonError("updated_by required");
    const perm = requireErpRole_(actor, ["ADMIN","OP","WH","WAREHOUSE"]);
    if (perm) return perm;

    const row = getOne("process_order_input","process_input_id",id);
    if(!row) return jsonError("Process input not found");
    const po = getOne("process_order","process_order_id",row.process_order_id);
    if(!po) return jsonError("Process order not found");
    const st = String(po.status || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    if(st === "CANCELLED") return jsonError("Process order is CANCELLED");

    updateGeneric("process_order_input", {
      process_input_id: id,
      remark: String(p && p.remark || ""),
      updated_by: actor,
      updated_at: p.updated_at || nowIso16()
    }, "process_input_id");
    return jsonSuccess({ message:"Updated" });
  }finally{
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}
