/**
 * Command：更新出貨明細備註（僅允許已過帳）
 * params: shipment_item_id, remark, updated_by, updated_at(optional)
 */
function updateShipmentItemRemark(p){
  const id = String(p && p.shipment_item_id || "").trim();
  const remark = String(p && p.remark || "");
  const actor = String(p && (p.updated_by || p.created_by) || "").trim();
  if(!id) return jsonError("shipment_item_id required");
  if(!actor) return jsonError("updated_by required");
  const perm = requireErpRole_(actor, ["ADMIN","OP","WH","WAREHOUSE"]);
  if (perm) return perm;

  const sheet = getSheet("shipment_item");
  if(!sheet) return jsonError("Sheet not found: shipment_item");
  const data = sheet.getDataRange().getDisplayValues();
  if(data.length < 2) return jsonError("No data");
  const headers = data[0];
  const idIdx = headers.indexOf("shipment_item_id");
  const sidIdx = headers.indexOf("shipment_id");
  const rmIdx = headers.indexOf("remark");
  const ubIdx = headers.indexOf("updated_by");
  const uaIdx = headers.indexOf("updated_at");
  if(idIdx === -1) return jsonError("shipment_item_id column not found");
  if(sidIdx === -1) return jsonError("shipment_id column not found");
  if(rmIdx === -1) return jsonError("remark column not found");

  for(let i=1;i<data.length;i++){
    if(String(data[i][idIdx] || "") !== id) continue;
    const shipmentId = String(data[i][sidIdx] || "").trim();
    const sh = getShipmentById_(shipmentId);
    if(!sh) return jsonError("Shipment not found: " + shipmentId);
    const st = String(sh.status || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    if(st !== "POSTED") return jsonError("Only POSTED shipment item remark can be updated");

    const oldObj = rowToObject(headers, data[i]);
    const oldDiff = { remark: oldObj.remark };
    const newDiff = { remark: remark };

    sheet.getRange(i+1, rmIdx+1).setValue(remark);
    if(ubIdx !== -1) sheet.getRange(i+1, ubIdx+1).setValue(actor);
    if(uaIdx !== -1) sheet.getRange(i+1, uaIdx+1).setValue(p.updated_at || nowIso16());

    try{
      writeLog("shipment_item", id, "UPDATE", oldDiff, newDiff, actor);
    }catch(_e){}
    return jsonSuccess({ message: "Updated" });
  }
  return jsonError("Record not found");
}

// #SEC:SHIPMENT
/**
 * Command：更新出貨主檔備註（不變更狀態／日期／客戶等；稽核用途）
 * params: shipment_id, remark, updated_by, updated_at(optional)
 */
function updateShipmentRemark(p){
  const id = String(p && p.shipment_id || "").trim();
  const remark = String(p && p.remark || "");
  const actor = String(p && (p.updated_by || p.created_by) || "").trim();
  if(!id) return jsonError("shipment_id required");
  if(!actor) return jsonError("updated_by required");
  const perm = requireErpRole_(actor, ["ADMIN","OP","WH","WAREHOUSE"]);
  if (perm) return perm;

  const sh0 = getShipmentById_(id);
  if(!sh0) return jsonError("Shipment not found: " + id);

  const sheet = getSheet("shipment");
  if(!sheet) return jsonError("Sheet not found: shipment");
  const data = sheet.getDataRange().getDisplayValues();
  if(data.length < 2) return jsonError("No data");
  const headers = data[0];
  const idIdx = headers.indexOf("shipment_id");
  const rmIdx = headers.indexOf("remark");
  const ubIdx = headers.indexOf("updated_by");
  const uaIdx = headers.indexOf("updated_at");
  if(idIdx === -1) return jsonError("shipment_id column not found");
  if(rmIdx === -1) return jsonError("remark column not found");

  for(let i=1;i<data.length;i++){
    if(String(data[i][idIdx] || "").trim() !== id) continue;
    const oldObj = rowToObject(headers, data[i]);
    const oldDiff = { remark: oldObj.remark };
    const newDiff = { remark: remark };

    sheet.getRange(i+1, rmIdx+1).setValue(remark);
    if(ubIdx !== -1) sheet.getRange(i+1, ubIdx+1).setValue(actor);
    if(uaIdx !== -1) sheet.getRange(i+1, uaIdx+1).setValue(p.updated_at || nowIso16());

    try{
      writeLog("shipment", id, "UPDATE", oldDiff, newDiff, actor);
    }catch(_e){}
    return jsonSuccess({ message: "Updated" });
  }
  return jsonError("Record not found");
}

/**
 * 出貨過帳（Bundle）
 * - 一次完成：shipment + shipment_item + inventory_movement(SHIP_OUT) + 回寫 sales_order_item.shipped_qty + sales_order.status
 * - 使用 ScriptLock 避免多人同時過帳造成 race condition
 *
 * params:
 * - shipment_id, so_id(optional), customer_id, ship_date, shipper_id, remark(optional), created_by, created_at(optional)
 * - parent_ref_type(optional): SO (default) | SHIPMENT
 * - parent_ref_id(optional): when parent_ref_type=SHIPMENT, set to original shipment_id
 * - items_json: JSON array [{ so_id?, so_item_id?, lot_id, product_id?, ship_qty, unit?, remark? }]
 */
function postShipmentBundle(p) {
  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  lock.waitLock(20000);

  try {
    const shipmentId = String(p.shipment_id || "").trim();
    if (!shipmentId) return jsonError("shipment_id required");
    const customerId = String(p.customer_id || "").trim();
    if (!customerId) return jsonError("customer_id required");
    const shipDate = String(p.ship_date || "").trim();
    if (!shipDate) return jsonError("ship_date required");
    const shipperId = String(p.shipper_id || "").trim();
    if (!shipperId) return jsonError("shipper_id required");
    const soId = String(p.so_id || "").trim();
    if (!soId) return jsonError("so_id required");
    const remark = String(p.remark || "");
    const actor = String(p.created_by || p.updated_by || "").trim();
    if (!actor) return jsonError("created_by required");
    const perm = requireErpRole_(actor, ["ADMIN","OP","WH","WAREHOUSE"]);
    if (perm) return perm;
    const expectedItems = Number(p.expected_existed_shipment_item_count || 0);
    if (isNaN(expectedItems)) return jsonError("expected_existed_shipment_item_count invalid");

    // parent_ref：預設 SO；可選 SHIPMENT（用於補寄/再出貨等 shipment → shipment 延伸）
    const parentRefType = String(p.parent_ref_type || "SO").trim().toUpperCase() || "SO";
    let parentRefId = String(p.parent_ref_id || "").trim();
    if(parentRefType !== "SO" && parentRefType !== "SHIPMENT"){
      return jsonError("parent_ref_type must be SO or SHIPMENT");
    }
    if(parentRefType === "SO"){
      parentRefId = soId;
    }else{
      if(!parentRefId) return jsonError("parent_ref_id required when parent_ref_type=SHIPMENT");
      const parentSh = getShipmentById_(parentRefId);
      if(!parentSh) return jsonError("Parent shipment not found: " + parentRefId);
      const pst = String(parentSh.status || "").trim().toUpperCase();
      if(pst === "CANCELLED") return jsonError("Parent shipment is CANCELLED: " + parentRefId);
      if(String(parentSh.so_id || "").trim() !== soId) return jsonError("parent shipment so_id mismatch");
    }

    // 防重：若 shipment 已存在且已 POSTED/CANCELLED，禁止再次過帳
    const existed = getShipmentById_(shipmentId);
    if (existed) {
      const st = String(existed.status || "").toUpperCase();
      if (st === "POSTED") return jsonError("Shipment already POSTED");
      if (st === "CANCELLED") return jsonError("Shipment already CANCELLED");
      return jsonError("Shipment already exists");
    }
    const existedItemCount = (function(){
      const sheet = getSheet("shipment_item");
      if(!sheet) return 0;
      const data = sheet.getDataRange().getDisplayValues();
      if(data.length < 2) return 0;
      const headers = data[0];
      const idxSid = headers.indexOf("shipment_id");
      if(idxSid === -1) return 0;
      let c = 0;
      for(let i=1;i<data.length;i++){
        if(String(data[i][idxSid] || "").trim().toUpperCase() === shipmentId) c++;
      }
      return c;
    })();
    if(existedItemCount !== expectedItems){
      return jsonError("Shipment items changed. Please reload and try again");
    }

    let items = [];
    try {
      items = JSON.parse(p.items_json || "[]");
    } catch (_e) {
      return jsonError("items_json must be valid JSON array");
    }
    if (!Array.isArray(items) || items.length === 0) return jsonError("Shipment items required");

    // transaction_id：沿用 SO；若 SO 尚未有 transaction_id 則補上
    const txId = ensureSalesOrderTx_(soId, actor);

    const idem = consumeIdempotencyKey_("POST_SHIPMENT_BUNDLE", p && p.idempotency_key, 600);
    if(!idem.ok) return jsonError("Duplicate request detected. Please reload and try again");

    // 先建立 shipment（固定 POSTED）
    var _failSh = bundleStepFail_(createGeneric("shipment", {
      _system_write: "1",
      shipment_id: shipmentId,
      so_id: soId,
      customer_id: customerId,
      shipper_id: shipperId,
      transaction_id: txId,
      parent_ref_type: parentRefType,
      parent_ref_id: parentRefId,
      ship_date: shipDate,
      status: "POSTED",
      remark: remark,
      created_by: actor,
      created_at: p.created_at || nowIso16(),
      updated_by: "",
      updated_at: ""
    }, "shipment_id"));
    if (_failSh) return _failSh;

    // 同一張出貨單可能多筆共用同一 so_item_id（FEFO 拆行）→ 先在記憶體累加，再一次次更新
    const shippedDeltaBySoItem = {};
    const touchedSoIds = {};

    for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
      const it = items[i] || {};
      const lotId = String(it.lot_id || "").trim();
      if (!lotId) return jsonError("lot_id required (items[" + i + "])");
      const q = Number(it.ship_qty || 0);
      if (!(q > 0)) return jsonError("ship_qty must be > 0 (items[" + i + "])");

      const itemSoId = String(it.so_id || soId || "").trim();
      const soItemId = String(it.so_item_id || "").trim();
      if (!itemSoId) return jsonError("so_id required (items[" + i + "])");
      if (!soItemId) return jsonError("so_item_id required (items[" + i + "])");
      if (itemSoId) touchedSoIds[itemSoId] = true;
      if (itemSoId !== soId) return jsonError("items[" + i + "].so_id must match shipment so_id");

      // lot 基本資料（倉別/產品/單位）
      const lot = getLotById_(lotId);
      if (!lot) return jsonError("Lot not found: " + lotId);

      const unit = String(it.unit || lot.unit || "").trim();
      const productId = String(it.product_id || lot.product_id || "").trim();

      // 1) shipment_item
      var _failSi = bundleStepFail_(createGeneric("shipment_item", {
        _system_write: "1",
        shipment_item_id: "SHI-" + shipmentId + "-" + String(i + 1).padStart(3, "0"),
        shipment_id: shipmentId,
        so_id: itemSoId,
        so_item_id: soItemId,
        lot_id: lotId,
        product_id: productId,
        transaction_id: txId,
        parent_ref_type: "SHIPMENT",
        parent_ref_id: shipmentId,
        ship_qty: String(q),
        unit: unit,
        remark: String(it.remark || ""),
        created_by: actor,
        created_at: nowIso16(),
        updated_by: "",
        updated_at: ""
      }, "shipment_item_id"));
      if (_failSi) return _failSi;

      // 2) movement: SHIP_OUT（負數）
      const mvRes = createInventoryMovementUnlocked_({
        movement_id: it.movement_id || ("MV-" + new Date().getTime() + "-" + i),
        movement_type: "SHIP_OUT",
        lot_id: lotId,
        product_id: productId,
        warehouse_id: String(lot.warehouse_id || "MAIN").trim().toUpperCase() || "MAIN",
        transaction_id: txId,
        parent_ref_type: "SHIPMENT",
        parent_ref_id: shipmentId,
        qty: String(-Math.abs(q)),
        unit: unit,
        ref_type: "SHIPMENT",
        ref_id: shipmentId,
        issued_to: "", // 保留欄位
        remark: "",
        created_by: actor,
        created_at: nowIso16(),
        updated_by: "",
        updated_at: "",
        system_remark: "Ship OUT: " + shipmentId
      });
      var _failMv = bundleStepFail_(mvRes);
      if (_failMv) return _failMv;

      // 3) 累加 SO shipped_qty（若有 so_item_id）
      if (soItemId) {
        shippedDeltaBySoItem[soItemId] = (shippedDeltaBySoItem[soItemId] || 0) + q;
      }
    }

    // 回寫 sales_order_item.shipped_qty
    const soItemIds = Object.keys(shippedDeltaBySoItem);
    for (let j = 0; j < soItemIds.length; j++) {
      const soItemId = soItemIds[j];
      const row = getSalesOrderItemById_(soItemId);
      if (!row) continue;
      const next = Number(row.shipped_qty || 0) + Number(shippedDeltaBySoItem[soItemId] || 0);
      var _failSoi = bundleStepFail_(updateGeneric("sales_order_item", {
        so_item_id: soItemId,
        shipped_qty: String(next),
        updated_by: actor,
        updated_at: nowIso16()
      }, "so_item_id"));
      if (_failSoi) return _failSoi;
    }

    // 回寫 sales_order.status（只處理 touched 的 so_id）
    const soIds = Object.keys(touchedSoIds);
    for (let k = 0; k < soIds.length; k++) {
      const sid = soIds[k];
      const rows = listSalesOrderItemsBySoId_(sid);
      const allShipped = rows.length > 0 && rows.every(function (x) {
        return Number(x.shipped_qty || 0) >= Number(x.order_qty || 0);
      });
      const anyShipped = rows.some(function (x) {
        return Number(x.shipped_qty || 0) > 0;
      });
      const nextStatus = allShipped ? "SHIPPED" : (anyShipped ? "PARTIAL" : "OPEN");
      var _failSo = bundleStepFail_(updateGeneric("sales_order", {
        so_id: sid,
        status: nextStatus,
        updated_by: actor,
        updated_at: nowIso16()
      }, "so_id"));
      if (_failSo) return _failSo;
    }

    writeAuditBundle_("shipment", shipmentId, "POST_SHIPMENT", { shipment_id: shipmentId, so_id: soId, item_count: items.length }, actor);
    return jsonSuccess({ message: "POSTED", shipment_id: shipmentId });
  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

/**
 * 出貨作廢（Bundle）
 * - 僅允許 POSTED → CANCELLED
 * - 一次完成：反沖庫存（ADJUST +qty, ref_type=SHIPMENT_CANCEL）+ shipment.status=CANCELLED + 回寫 SO shipped_qty + SO status
 */
function cancelShipmentBundle(p) {
  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  lock.waitLock(20000);

  try {
    const shipmentId = String(p.shipment_id || "").trim();
    if (!shipmentId) return jsonError("shipment_id required");
    const actor = String(p.updated_by || p.created_by || "").trim();
    if (!actor) return jsonError("updated_by required");
    const perm = requireErpRole_(actor, ["ADMIN","OP","WH","WAREHOUSE"]);
    if (perm) return perm;

    const sh = getShipmentById_(shipmentId);
    if (!sh) return jsonError("Shipment not found");
    const txId = normalizeTxId_(sh.transaction_id) || ensureSalesOrderTx_(String(sh.so_id || ""), actor) || buildTxId_();

    const st = String(sh.status || "").toUpperCase();
    if (st === "CANCELLED") return jsonError("Shipment already CANCELLED");
    if (st !== "POSTED") return jsonError("Only POSTED shipment can be cancelled");

    // 讀取明細
    const itemsSheet = getSheet("shipment_item");
    if (!itemsSheet) return jsonError("Sheet not found: shipment_item");
    const data = itemsSheet.getDataRange().getDisplayValues();
    if (data.length < 2) return jsonError("Shipment items not found");
    const headers = data[0];
    const idxShipmentId = headers.indexOf("shipment_id");
    if (idxShipmentId === -1) throw new Error("shipment_id column not found in shipment_item");

    const items = [];
    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (String(data[i][idxShipmentId] || "") === shipmentId) {
        items.push(rowToObject(headers, data[i]));
      }
    }
    if (items.length === 0) return jsonError("Shipment items not found");

    // 防重：若已存在 SHIPMENT_CANCEL movement（ref_id=shipmentId）→ 不再重複反沖
    const mvSheet = getSheet("inventory_movement");
    if (!mvSheet) return jsonError("Sheet not found: inventory_movement");
    const mvData = mvSheet.getDataRange().getDisplayValues();
    if (mvData.length >= 2) {
      const mvHeaders = mvData[0];
      const idxRefType = mvHeaders.indexOf("ref_type");
      const idxRefId = mvHeaders.indexOf("ref_id");
      if (idxRefType !== -1 && idxRefId !== -1) {
        for (let r = 1; r < mvData.length; r++) {
          if (
            String(mvData[r][idxRefType] || "").toUpperCase() === "SHIPMENT_CANCEL" &&
            String(mvData[r][idxRefId] || "") === shipmentId
          ) {
            return jsonError("Shipment cancel movement already exists");
          }
        }
      }
    }

    // 全部驗證通過後才進入 idempotency gate（避免重送重寫）
    const idem = consumeIdempotencyKey_("CANCEL_SHIPMENT_BUNDLE", p && p.idempotency_key, 600);
    if(!idem.ok) return jsonError("Duplicate request detected. Please reload and try again");

    // 1) 反沖庫存：ADJUST +qty（不做 QA gating；createInventoryMovementUnlocked_ 只對 OUT/PROCESS_OUT/SHIP_OUT 做 gating）
    const shippedDeltaBySoItem = {};
    const touchedSoIds = {};

    for (let j = 0; j < items.length; j++) {
      const it = items[j] || {};
      const lotId = String(it.lot_id || "").trim();
      const q = Number(it.ship_qty || 0);
      if (!lotId) continue;
      if (!(q > 0)) continue;

      const lot = getLotById_(lotId);
      if (!lot) return jsonError("Lot not found: " + lotId);

      const unit = String(it.unit || lot.unit || "").trim();
      const productId = String(it.product_id || lot.product_id || "").trim();

      const mvRes = createInventoryMovementUnlocked_({
        movement_id: "MV-" + new Date().getTime() + "-C" + j,
        movement_type: "ADJUST",
        lot_id: lotId,
        product_id: productId,
        warehouse_id: String(lot.warehouse_id || "MAIN").trim().toUpperCase() || "MAIN",
        transaction_id: txId,
        parent_ref_type: "SHIPMENT",
        parent_ref_id: shipmentId,
        qty: String(Math.abs(q)),
        unit: unit,
        ref_type: "SHIPMENT_CANCEL",
        ref_id: shipmentId,
        issued_to: "",
        remark: "",
        created_by: actor,
        created_at: nowIso16(),
        updated_by: "",
        updated_at: "",
        system_remark: "Cancel Shipment: " + shipmentId
      });
      var _failCmv = bundleStepFail_(mvRes);
      if (_failCmv) return _failCmv;

      const soItemId = String(it.so_item_id || "").trim();
      const soId = String(it.so_id || "").trim();
      if (soId) touchedSoIds[soId] = true;
      if (soItemId) shippedDeltaBySoItem[soItemId] = (shippedDeltaBySoItem[soItemId] || 0) + q;
    }

    // 2) shipment.status = CANCELLED（保留原 remark，加上 CANCELLED 註記）
    const prevRemark = String(sh.remark || "").trim();
    const nextRemark = prevRemark ? (prevRemark + " | CANCELLED") : "CANCELLED";
    var _failCsh = bundleStepFail_(updateGeneric("shipment", {
      shipment_id: shipmentId,
      status: "CANCELLED",
      remark: nextRemark,
      updated_by: actor,
      updated_at: nowIso16(),
      _system_write: "1"
    }, "shipment_id"));
    if (_failCsh) return _failCsh;

    // 3) 回寫 sales_order_item.shipped_qty（扣回）
    const soItemIds = Object.keys(shippedDeltaBySoItem);
    for (let k = 0; k < soItemIds.length; k++) {
      const soItemId = soItemIds[k];
      const row = getSalesOrderItemById_(soItemId);
      if (!row) continue;
      const next = Math.max(0, Number(row.shipped_qty || 0) - Number(shippedDeltaBySoItem[soItemId] || 0));
      var _failCsoi = bundleStepFail_(updateGeneric("sales_order_item", {
        so_item_id: soItemId,
        shipped_qty: String(next),
        updated_by: actor,
        updated_at: nowIso16()
      }, "so_item_id"));
      if (_failCsoi) return _failCsoi;
    }

    // 4) 回寫 sales_order.status（依 touched 的 so_id）
    const soIds = Object.keys(touchedSoIds);
    for (let m = 0; m < soIds.length; m++) {
      const sid = soIds[m];
      const rows = listSalesOrderItemsBySoId_(sid);
      const allShipped = rows.length > 0 && rows.every(function (x) {
        return Number(x.shipped_qty || 0) >= Number(x.order_qty || 0);
      });
      const anyShipped = rows.some(function (x) {
        return Number(x.shipped_qty || 0) > 0;
      });
      const nextStatus = allShipped ? "SHIPPED" : (anyShipped ? "PARTIAL" : "OPEN");
      var _failCso = bundleStepFail_(updateGeneric("sales_order", {
        so_id: sid,
        status: nextStatus,
        updated_by: actor,
        updated_at: nowIso16()
      }, "so_id"));
      if (_failCso) return _failCso;
    }

    writeAuditBundle_("shipment", shipmentId, "CANCEL_SHIPMENT", { shipment_id: shipmentId }, actor);
    return jsonSuccess({ message: "CANCELLED", shipment_id: shipmentId });
  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

/* =================================================
   CANCEL DOC BUNDLES（僅改狀態 + 系統註記）
================================================= */

function hasActiveGoodsReceiptByPoId_(poId) {
  const id = String(poId || "").trim();
  if (!id) return false;
  const sheet = getSheet("goods_receipt");
  if (!sheet) return false;
  const data = sheet.getDataRange().getDisplayValues();
  if (data.length < 2) return false;
  const headers = data[0];
  const idxPo = headers.indexOf("po_id");
  const idxSt = headers.indexOf("status");
  if (idxPo === -1) throw new Error("po_id column not found in goods_receipt");
  for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (String(data[i][idxPo] || "") !== id) continue;
    const st = String(idxSt >= 0 ? (data[i][idxSt] || "") : "").toUpperCase();
    if (st !== "CANCELLED") return true;
  }
  return false;
}

function hasActiveImportReceiptByDocId_(docId) {
  const id = String(docId || "").trim();
  if (!id) return false;
  const sheet = getSheet("import_receipt");
  if (!sheet) return false;
  const data = sheet.getDataRange().getDisplayValues();
  if (data.length < 2) return false;
  const headers = data[0];
  const idxDoc = headers.indexOf("import_doc_id");
  const idxSt = headers.indexOf("status");
  if (idxDoc === -1) throw new Error("import_doc_id column not found in import_receipt");
  for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (String(data[i][idxDoc] || "") !== id) continue;
    const st = String(idxSt >= 0 ? (data[i][idxSt] || "") : "").toUpperCase();
    if (st !== "CANCELLED") return true;
  }
  return false;
}

function hasActiveShipmentBySoId_(soId) {
  const id = String(soId || "").trim();
  if (!id) return false;
  const sheet = getSheet("shipment");
  if (!sheet) return false;
  const data = sheet.getDataRange().getDisplayValues();
  if (data.length < 2) return false;
  const headers = data[0];
  const idxSo = headers.indexOf("so_id");
  const idxSt = headers.indexOf("status");
  if (idxSo === -1) throw new Error("so_id column not found in shipment");
  for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (String(data[i][idxSo] || "") !== id) continue;
    const st = String(idxSt >= 0 ? (data[i][idxSt] || "") : "").toUpperCase();
    if (st !== "CANCELLED") return true;
  }
  return false;
}

function cancelPurchaseOrderBundle(p) {
  const poId = String(p.po_id || "").trim();
  if (!poId) return jsonError("po_id required");
  const actor = String(p.updated_by || p.created_by || "").trim();
  if (!actor) return jsonError("updated_by required");
  const perm = requireErpRole_(actor, ["ADMIN","OP","WH","WAREHOUSE"]);
  if (perm) return perm;

  const po = getOne("purchase_order", "po_id", poId);
  if (!po) return jsonError("Purchase order not found");
  if (String(po.status || "").toUpperCase() === "CANCELLED") return jsonError("Purchase order already CANCELLED");
  if (hasActiveGoodsReceiptByPoId_(poId)) return jsonError("Cannot cancel: active goods receipts exist");

  // 全部驗證通過後才進入 idempotency gate（避免重送重寫）
  const idem = consumeIdempotencyKey_("CANCEL_PURCHASE_ORDER_BUNDLE", p && p.idempotency_key, 600);
  if(!idem.ok) return jsonError("Duplicate request detected. Please reload and try again");

  const note = String(p.cancel_note || "").trim();
  const line = "[作廢 " + nowIso16() + " " + actor + "] " + (note || "");
  var _failPo = bundleStepFail_(updateGeneric("purchase_order", {
    _system_write: "1",
    po_id: poId,
    status: "CANCELLED",
    system_remark: appendSystemRemark_(po.system_remark, line.trim()),
    updated_by: actor,
    updated_at: nowIso16()
  }, "po_id"));
  if (_failPo) return _failPo;

  return jsonSuccess({ message: "CANCELLED", po_id: poId });
}

function cancelImportDocumentBundle(p) {
  const docId = String(p.import_doc_id || "").trim();
  if (!docId) return jsonError("import_doc_id required");
  const actor = String(p.updated_by || p.created_by || "").trim();
  if (!actor) return jsonError("updated_by required");
  const perm = requireErpRole_(actor, ["ADMIN","OP","WH","WAREHOUSE"]);
  if (perm) return perm;

  const doc = getOne("import_document", "import_doc_id", docId);
  if (!doc) return jsonError("Import document not found");
  if (String(doc.status || "").toUpperCase() === "CANCELLED") return jsonError("Import document already CANCELLED");
  if (hasActiveImportReceiptByDocId_(docId)) return jsonError("Cannot cancel: active import receipts exist");

  // 全部驗證通過後才進入 idempotency gate（避免重送重寫）
  const idem = consumeIdempotencyKey_("CANCEL_IMPORT_DOCUMENT_BUNDLE", p && p.idempotency_key, 600);
  if(!idem.ok) return jsonError("Duplicate request detected. Please reload and try again");

  const note = String(p.cancel_note || "").trim();
  const line = "[作廢 " + nowIso16() + " " + actor + "] " + (note || "");
  var _failDoc = bundleStepFail_(updateGeneric("import_document", {
    _system_write: "1",
    import_doc_id: docId,
    status: "CANCELLED",
    system_remark: appendSystemRemark_(doc.system_remark, line.trim()),
    updated_by: actor,
    updated_at: nowIso16()
  }, "import_doc_id"));
  if (_failDoc) return _failDoc;

  return jsonSuccess({ message: "CANCELLED", import_doc_id: docId });
}

function cancelSalesOrderBundle(p) {
  const soId = String(p.so_id || "").trim();
  if (!soId) return jsonError("so_id required");
  const actor = String(p.updated_by || p.created_by || "").trim();
  if (!actor) return jsonError("updated_by required");
  const perm = requireErpRole_(actor, ["ADMIN","OP","WH","WAREHOUSE"]);
  if (perm) return perm;

  const so = getOne("sales_order", "so_id", soId);
  if (!so) return jsonError("Sales order not found");
  if (String(so.status || "").toUpperCase() === "CANCELLED") return jsonError("Sales order already CANCELLED");
  if (hasActiveShipmentBySoId_(soId)) return jsonError("Cannot cancel: active shipments exist");

  // 全部驗證通過後才進入 idempotency gate（避免重送重寫）
  const idem = consumeIdempotencyKey_("CANCEL_SALES_ORDER_BUNDLE", p && p.idempotency_key, 600);
  if(!idem.ok) return jsonError("Duplicate request detected. Please reload and try again");

  const note = String(p.cancel_note || "").trim();
  const line = "[作廢 " + nowIso16() + " " + actor + "] " + (note || "");
  var _failSo = bundleStepFail_(updateGeneric("sales_order", {
    _system_write: "1",
    so_id: soId,
    status: "CANCELLED",
    system_remark: appendSystemRemark_(so.system_remark, line.trim()),
    updated_by: actor,
    updated_at: nowIso16()
  }, "so_id"));
  if (_failSo) return _failSo;

  return jsonSuccess({ message: "CANCELLED", so_id: soId });
}

// #SEC:AUTH
/* =================================================
   USERS
================================================= */

// =================================================
// ADMIN（救火帳密，避免出現在 Google Sheet）
// - 目的：Users Sheet 不放 ADMIN，避免被看到/誤改
// - 注意：請勿把明碼寫在程式裡；只放 SHA-256 HEX
// - 用法：
//   1) 在 Apps Script 編輯器執行 devHashPassword_("你的密碼") 取得 HEX
//   2) 把 ERP_ADMIN_PASSWORD_SHA256_HEX_ 換成輸出的 HEX
// =================================================
var ERP_ADMIN_USER_ID_ = "admin";
var ERP_ADMIN_USER_NAME_ = "ADMIN";
// 安全預設：不在程式碼內放固定 hash；未設定時即停用帳密登入
// - 可用 Script Properties 設定：ERP_ADMIN_PASSWORD_SHA256_HEX（建議）
// - 或自行將下方變數改成你的 SHA-256 HEX（不建議在多人可讀環境）
var ERP_ADMIN_PASSWORD_SHA256_HEX_ = "";

function devHashPassword_(pw){
  var s = String(pw == null ? "" : pw);
  var bytes = Utilities.newBlob(s, "text/plain").getBytes();
  var dig = Utilities.computeDigest(Utilities.DigestAlgorithm.SHA_256, bytes);
  var out = "";
  for(var i=0;i<dig.length;i++){
    var v = (dig[i] < 0 ? dig[i] + 256 : dig[i]);
    var h = v.toString(16);
    if(h.length < 2) h = "0" + h;
    out += h;
  }
  return out;
}

/**
 * 免參數：把暫存明碼（Script Properties）轉成 SHA-256 HEX 並輸出到 Logger
 * 用法：
 * 1) Apps Script → 專案設定 → 指令碼屬性（Script properties）新增
 *    Key: ERP_ADMIN_PW_PLAIN_TMP
 *    Value: 你的 ADMIN 密碼（只放一次，用完立刻刪掉）
 * 2) 執行 devPrintAdminPwHash_()
 * 3) 看執行記錄/日誌（Logs）→ 複製輸出的 HEX，貼回 ERP_ADMIN_PASSWORD_SHA256_HEX_
 * 4) 刪除 Script Properties 的 ERP_ADMIN_PW_PLAIN_TMP
 */
function devPrintAdminPwHash_(){
  var props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  var plain = String(props.getProperty("ERP_ADMIN_PW_PLAIN_TMP") || "");
  if(!plain) throw new Error("Missing Script Property: ERP_ADMIN_PW_PLAIN_TMP");
  var hex = devHashPassword_(plain);
  Logger.log(hex);
  return hex;
}

// Apps Script 執行下拉有時不顯示底線結尾函式；提供無底線包裝，方便直接執行
function devPrintAdminPwHash(){
  return devPrintAdminPwHash_();
}

function verifyAdminPassword_(plainPw){
  var want = "";
  try{
    want = String(PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("ERP_ADMIN_PASSWORD_SHA256_HEX") || "").trim();
  }catch(_e){ want = ""; }
  if(!want) want = String(ERP_ADMIN_PASSWORD_SHA256_HEX_ || "").trim();
  if(!want) return false;
  var got = String(devHashPassword_(plainPw) || "").trim().toLowerCase();
  return got === want;
}

function isBuiltinAdmin_(userId){
  var id = String(userId || "").trim();
  var a = String(ERP_ADMIN_USER_ID_ || "").trim();
  if(!id || !a) return false;
  return id.toUpperCase() === a.toUpperCase();
}

  return listGeneric("user");
}

  return createGeneric("user", p, "user_id");
}

function updateUser(p) {
  return updateGeneric("user", p, "user_id");
}

function deleteUser(p) {

}

/**
 * 回傳環境資訊（供前端維運用途）
 * - 權限：僅 CEO/GA/ADMIN
 * - 回傳包含：env、spreadsheet_id
 */
function envInfo(p){
  const actor = String(p && (p.updated_by || p.created_by) || "").trim();
  const perm = requireErpRole_(actor, ["CEO","GA","ADMIN"]);
  if (perm) return perm;
  return jsonSuccess({
    spreadsheet_id: getSpreadsheetId_(),
    sheet_gids: getSheetGids_()
  });
}

/**
 * Login（帳號密碼）
 * - 用途：前端登入驗證（避免把 Users/密碼整包下發）
 * - 規則：user_id 存在、ACTIVE、密碼吻合
 * - remember_me：1 / true = 長效 session（30 天，滑動續期）；否則 8 小時
 * - 成功回傳：session_token + session_expires_at（不含 password）
 * - 已廢除 password="__SESSION__"，請改用 action session_resume
 */
function loginUser(p){
  const userIdRaw = String(p && p.user_id || "").trim();
  const pw = String(p && p.password || "");
  if(!userIdRaw) return jsonError("user_id required");
  if(pw === undefined) return jsonError("password required");

  if(String(pw) === "__SESSION__"){
    return jsonError("USE_SESSION_RESUME");
  }

  // 帳密登入：僅允許程式內建的 ADMIN 救火（不依賴 Users Sheet）
  const wantU = userIdRaw.toUpperCase();
  const adminId = String(ERP_ADMIN_USER_ID_ || "").trim();
  if(adminId && wantU === adminId.toUpperCase()){
    if(!verifyAdminPassword_(pw)) return jsonError("BAD_PASSWORD");
    const rememberMe = String(p && p.remember_me || "");
    const sess = createSession_(adminId, ERP_ADMIN_USER_NAME_, "ADMIN", rememberMe, "*");
    var tz = "Asia/Taipei";
    try{ tz = Session.getScriptTimeZone() || tz; }catch(_tz){}
    const expIso = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(Number(sess.exp)), tz, "yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ss");
    return jsonSuccess({
      user_id: adminId,
      user_name: ERP_ADMIN_USER_NAME_,
      role: "ADMIN",
      allowed_modules: "*",
      status: "ACTIVE",
      remember: !!sess.record.remember,
      session_token: sess.token,
      session_expires_at: expIso
    });
  }

  // 其餘角色：一律走 Google 登入（名單制）
  return jsonError("PASSWORD_LOGIN_DISABLED");
}

/**
 * Google Login（名單制）
 * - 前端先用 Google Sign-In 取得 id_token，再送到此 action
 * - 後端驗證：id_token 有效 + aud === GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID + email_verified
 * - 再以 email 對照 user 表（只允許名單內且 ACTIVE）
 * - 成功後回傳 session_token（沿用既有 session 機制）
 */
function googleLogin(p){
  const idToken = String(p && p.id_token || "").trim();
  const rememberMe = String(p && p.remember_me || "");
  if(!idToken) return jsonError("BAD_ID_TOKEN");

  let info;
  try{
    const url = "https://oauth2.googleapis.com/tokeninfo?id_token=" + encodeURIComponent(idToken);
    const resp = UrlFetchApp.fetch(url, { muteHttpExceptions: true });
    const code = resp.getResponseCode();
    const txt = resp.getContentText();
    if(code !== 200) return jsonError("BAD_ID_TOKEN");
    info = JSON.parse(txt);
  }catch(_e){
    return jsonError("BAD_ID_TOKEN");
  }

  const aud = String(info && info.aud || "").trim();
  if(!aud || aud !== GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID) return jsonError("BAD_AUD");
  const emailVerified = String(info && info.email_verified || "").trim().toLowerCase();
  if(emailVerified !== "true") return jsonError("BAD_ID_TOKEN");
  const email = String(info && info.email || "").trim().toLowerCase();
  if(!email) return jsonError("BAD_ID_TOKEN");

  const u = getActiveUserByEmail_(email);
  if(!u) return jsonError("NOT_ALLOWED");

  const sess = createSession_(u.user_id, u.user_name, u.role, rememberMe, u.allowed_modules);
  var tz = "Asia/Taipei";
  try{ tz = Session.getScriptTimeZone() || tz; }catch(_tz){}
  const expIso = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(Number(sess.exp)), tz, "yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ss");
  return jsonSuccess({
    user_id: u.user_id,
    user_name: u.user_name,
    role: u.role,
    allowed_modules: String(u.allowed_modules || ""),
    status: "ACTIVE",
    remember: !!sess.record.remember,
    session_token: sess.token,
    session_expires_at: expIso
  });
}

/**
 * 供首次部署後「手動授權 UrlFetch」用（避免 google_login 因未授權而回 BAD_ID_TOKEN）
 * - 在 Apps Script 編輯器點「執行」此函式一次，完成授權後即可刪除或保留
 */
function dev_auth_urlfetch(){
  const resp = UrlFetchApp.fetch("https://oauth2.googleapis.com/tokeninfo", { muteHttpExceptions: true });
  return jsonSuccess({ ok:true, status: resp.getResponseCode() });
}

function getActiveUserByEmail_(emailLower){
  const em = String(emailLower || "").trim().toLowerCase();
  if(!em) return null;
  const sheet = getSheet("user");
  if(!sheet) return null;
  const data = sheet.getDataRange().getDisplayValues();
  if(data.length < 2) return null;
  const headers = data[0];
  const idxEmail = headers.indexOf("email");
  const idxId = headers.indexOf("user_id");
  const idxName = headers.indexOf("user_name");
  const idxRole = headers.indexOf("role");
  const idxStatus = headers.indexOf("status");
  const idxAllowed = headers.indexOf("allowed_modules");
  if(idxEmail === -1 || idxId === -1) return null;
  for(let i=1;i<data.length;i++){
    const row = data[i];
    const e = String(row[idxEmail] || "").trim().toLowerCase();
    if(!e) continue;
    if(e !== em) continue;
    const st = idxStatus !== -1 ? String(row[idxStatus] || "").trim().toUpperCase() : "ACTIVE";
    if(st !== "ACTIVE") return null;
    return {
      user_id: String(row[idxId] || "").trim(),
      user_name: idxName !== -1 ? String(row[idxName] || "") : "",
      role: idxRole !== -1 ? String(row[idxRole] || "") : "",
      allowed_modules: idxAllowed !== -1 ? String(row[idxAllowed] || "") : ""
    };
  }
  return null;
}

/**
 * 主檔軟刪除（VOID）：product/supplier/customer/warehouse/user
 * - 不刪列，改寫 status=VOID + updated_at
 * - 仍寫入 DELETE log（old_value 保留整列）
 */
function softDeleteMaster_(sheetName, params, idField) {
  const idVal = String(params && params[idField] || "").trim();
  if (!idVal) return jsonError(idField + " required");

  const sheet = getSheet(sheetName);
  if (!sheet) return jsonError("Sheet not found: " + sheetName);

  const data = sheet.getDataRange().getDisplayValues();
  if (data.length < 2) return jsonError("No data");

  const headers = data[0];
  const idIndex = headers.indexOf(idField);
  const statusIdx = headers.indexOf("status");
  if (idIndex === -1) return jsonError("ID field not found");
  if (statusIdx === -1) return jsonError("status field not found: " + sheetName);

  const actor = String(params.updated_by || params.created_by || "").trim();
  const now = nowIso16();

  for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (String(data[i][idIndex] || "") === idVal) {
      const oldObj = rowToObject(headers, data[i]);
      try {
        writeLog(sheetName, idVal, "DELETE", oldObj, null, actor);
      } catch (_e) {}

      // 若已 VOID：視為成功（冪等）
      const curStatus = String(oldObj.status || "").trim().toUpperCase();
      if (curStatus === "VOID") return jsonSuccess({ message: "Deleted" });

      return updateGeneric(sheetName, {
        [idField]: idVal,
        status: "VOID",
        updated_by: actor,
        updated_at: now
      }, idField);
    }
  }
  return jsonError("Record not found");
}

/* =================================================
   LOGS
   只讀，用於 Logs 列表畫面
================================================= */

function listLogs() {
  return listGeneric(LOG_SHEET_NAME);
}

function listLogsRecent(p) {
  const days = Number(p && p.days);
  const limit = Number(p && p.limit || 2000);
  const cap = isNaN(limit) ? 2000 : Math.min(Math.max(limit, 50), 5000);
  const sheet = getLogSheet();
  if (!sheet) return jsonSuccess({ data: [] });
  const data = sheet.getDataRange().getDisplayValues();
  if (data.length < 2) return jsonSuccess({ data: [] });
  const headers = data[0];
  const idxAt = headers.indexOf("created_at");
  const cutoff = logsCutoffIso16_(isNaN(days) ? 90 : days);

  const out = [];
  for (let i = data.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
    if (out.length >= cap) break;
    const row = data[i];
    if (idxAt >= 0) {
      const t = normalizeLogIso16_(row[idxAt]);
      if (t && t < cutoff) continue;
    }
    out.push(rowToObject(headers, row));
  }
  return jsonSuccess({ data: out });
}

function listLogsByRef(p) {
  const tableName = String(p && p.table_name || "").trim();
  const referenceId = String(p && p.reference_id || "").trim();
  if (!referenceId) return jsonError("reference_id required");
  const days = Number(p && p.days);
  const limit = Number(p && p.limit || 500);
  const cap = isNaN(limit) ? 500 : Math.min(Math.max(limit, 10), 2000);
  const sheet = getLogSheet();
  if (!sheet) return jsonSuccess({ data: [] });
  const data = sheet.getDataRange().getDisplayValues();
  if (data.length < 2) return jsonSuccess({ data: [] });
  const headers = data[0];
  const idxTable = headers.indexOf("table_name");
  const idxRef = headers.indexOf("reference_id");
  const idxAt = headers.indexOf("created_at");
  if (idxRef === -1) return jsonError("reference_id column not found in logs");
  const cutoff = logsCutoffIso16_(isNaN(days) ? 3650 : days);
  const refLower = referenceId.toLowerCase();

  const out = [];
  for (let i = data.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
    if (out.length >= cap) break;
    const row = data[i];
    const rid = String(row[idxRef] || "").trim();
    if (!rid || rid.toLowerCase().indexOf(refLower) === -1) continue;
    if (tableName && idxTable >= 0 && String(row[idxTable] || "").trim() !== tableName) continue;
    if (idxAt >= 0) {
      const t = normalizeLogIso16_(row[idxAt]);
      if (t && t < cutoff) continue;
    }
    out.push(rowToObject(headers, row));
  }
  return jsonSuccess({ data: out });
}

/** 由 user 表查詢角色（找不到或非 ACTIVE 視為空） */
function getUserRoleById_(userId) {
  const id = String(userId || "").trim();
  if (!id) return "";
  const sheet = getSheet("user");
  if (!sheet) return "";
  const data = sheet.getDataRange().getDisplayValues();
  if (data.length < 2) return "";
  const headers = data[0];
  const idxId = headers.indexOf("user_id");
  const idxRole = headers.indexOf("role");
  const idxStatus = headers.indexOf("status");
  if (idxId === -1 || idxRole === -1) return "";
  for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (String(data[i][idxId] || "").trim() === id) {
      const st = idxStatus !== -1 ? String(data[i][idxStatus] || "").trim().toUpperCase() : "ACTIVE";
      if (st && st !== "ACTIVE") return "";
      return String(data[i][idxRole] || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    }
  }
  return "";
}

/** 由 user 表查詢 allowed_modules（空白視為全開；找不到或非 ACTIVE 視為空字串） */
function getUserAllowedModulesById_(userId) {
  const id = String(userId || "").trim();
  if (!id) return "";
  const sheet = getSheet("user");
  if (!sheet) return "";
  const data = sheet.getDataRange().getDisplayValues();
  if (data.length < 2) return "";
  const headers = data[0];
  const idxId = headers.indexOf("user_id");
  const idxAllowed = headers.indexOf("allowed_modules");
  const idxStatus = headers.indexOf("status");
  if (idxId === -1) return "";
  for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (String(data[i][idxId] || "").trim() === id) {
      const st = idxStatus !== -1 ? String(data[i][idxStatus] || "").trim().toUpperCase() : "ACTIVE";
      if (st && st !== "ACTIVE") return "";
      if (idxAllowed === -1) return "";
      return String(data[i][idxAllowed] || "").trim();
    }
  }
  return "";
}

function actionToModuleKey_(action){
  const a = String(action || "").trim().toLowerCase();
  if(!a) return "";
  if(a === "list_logs" || a === "list_logs_recent" || a === "list_logs_by_ref" || a.indexOf("fix_logs_") === 0) return "logs";
  if(a.indexOf("_product") !== -1) return "products";
  if(a.indexOf("_supplier") !== -1) return "suppliers";
  if(a.indexOf("_customer") !== -1) return "customers";
  if(a.indexOf("_warehouse") !== -1) return "warehouses";
  if(a.indexOf("_user") !== -1) return "users";
  if(a.indexOf("purchase_order") !== -1) return "purchase";
  if(a.indexOf("import_") !== -1) return "import";
  if(a.indexOf("goods_receipt") !== -1) return "receive";
  if(a.indexOf("_lot") !== -1) return "lots";
  if(a.indexOf("inventory_movement") !== -1) return "movements";
  if(a.indexOf("process_") !== -1 || a.indexOf("outsource") !== -1) return "outsource";
  if(a.indexOf("sales_order") !== -1) return "sales";
  if(a.indexOf("shipment") !== -1) return "shipping";
  if(a.indexOf("trace_") === 0) return "trace";
  if(a.indexOf("warehouse_stock") !== -1) return "warehouse_stock";
  if(a === "dev_clear_non_master") return "dashboard";
  return ""; // 未對應者暫不做模組限制
}

function hasModuleAccess_(userId, moduleKey){
  const m = String(moduleKey || "").trim();
  if(!m) return true;
  if(isBuiltinAdmin_(userId)) return true;
  // Dashboard / Trace：預設所有人可看
  if(m === "dashboard" || m === "trace") return true;
  // Users / Logs：預設只有 CEO / GA / ADMIN 可看
  if(m === "users"){
    const r0 = String(getUserRoleById_(userId) || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    return r0 === "CEO" || r0 === "GA" || r0 === "ADMIN";
  }
  // Logs：預設只有 CEO / GA / ADMIN 可看
  if(m === "logs"){
    const r = String(getUserRoleById_(userId) || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    return r === "CEO" || r === "GA" || r === "ADMIN";
  }
  const raw = String(getUserAllowedModulesById_(userId) || "").trim();
  // 預設：空白 = 全關（Dashboard/Trace/Logs 已在上方處理）
  if(!raw) return false;
  if(raw === "*" || String(raw).trim().toUpperCase() === "ALL") return true; // 相容舊資料
  const set = {};
  raw.split(",").map(s=>String(s||"").trim()).filter(Boolean).forEach(k=>{ set[k]=true; });
  return !!set[m];
}

/* =================================================
   SESSION TOKEN（伺服器通行證，Script Properties）
   - 保持登入：約 30 天（每次 session_resume 滑動續期）
   - 未保持登入：約 8 小時（每次 resume 滑動續期）
================================================= */

var ERP_SESSION_PREFIX_ = "ERPSESS_";

function erpSessionDurationMs_(remember) {
  var r = String(remember || "") === "1" || String(remember || "").toLowerCase() === "true";
  return r ? (30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000) : (8 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
}

function copyWebParams_(p) {
  var out = {};
  if (!p) return out;
  var k;
  for (k in p) {
    if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(p, k)) out[k] = p[k];
  }
  return out;
}

function createSession_(userId, userName, role, rememberParam, allowedModules) {
  var remember = String(rememberParam || "") === "1" || String(rememberParam || "").toLowerCase() === "true";
  var token =
    Utilities.getUuid().replace(/-/g, "") + Utilities.getUuid().replace(/-/g, "").substring(0, 12);
  var exp = Date.now() + erpSessionDurationMs_(remember ? "1" : "0");
  var rec = {
    user_id: String(userId || "").trim(),
    user_name: String(userName || ""),
    role: String(role || "").trim().toUpperCase(),
    allowed_modules: String(allowedModules || ""),
    remember: remember,
    exp: exp
  };
  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty(
    ERP_SESSION_PREFIX_ + token,
    JSON.stringify(rec)
  );
  return { token: token, exp: exp, record: rec };
}

function readSessionValid_(token) {
  var t = String(token || "").trim();
  if (!t) return null;
  var raw = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty(ERP_SESSION_PREFIX_ + t);
  if (!raw) return null;
  var o;
  try {
    o = JSON.parse(raw);
  } catch (e1) {
    return null;
  }
  if (!o || !o.user_id) return null;
  if (Date.now() > Number(o.exp || 0)) {
    PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().deleteProperty(ERP_SESSION_PREFIX_ + t);
    return null;
  }
  return o;
}

function deleteSession_(token) {
  var t = String(token || "").trim();
  if (!t) return;
  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().deleteProperty(ERP_SESSION_PREFIX_ + t);
}

function touchSession_(token) {
  var o = readSessionValid_(token);
  if (!o) return null;
  var remember = !!o.remember;
  o.exp = Date.now() + erpSessionDurationMs_(remember ? "1" : "0");
  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty(
    ERP_SESSION_PREFIX_ + String(token || "").trim(),
    JSON.stringify(o)
  );
  return o;
}

/* =================================================
   DRIVE BACKUP（每日自動備份 Spreadsheet 副本）
   - 目的：防誤刪/誤改/批次寫入錯誤，可快速回復到某一天
   - 建議：每日 1 次，保留 30 份（約 30 天）
   - 用法：在 Apps Script「觸發條件」設定每天執行 backupErpDbDaily()
================================================= */

var ERP_BACKUP_FOLDER_ID = "1O9JiKTUSM7cdsFLGTPXc9dNXU8Z39Y0B";
var ERP_BACKUP_KEEP_COPIES = 30;
var ERP_BACKUP_NAME_PREFIX_ = "ERP_DB_Backup_";

/**
 * 每日備份：把整份 ERP 試算表複製到指定資料夾
 * 檔名：ERP_DB_Backup_YYYY-MM-DD_HHMM
 */
function backupErpDbDaily() {
  var folderId = String(ERP_BACKUP_FOLDER_ID || "").trim();
  if (!folderId) throw new Error("ERP_BACKUP_FOLDER_ID missing");

  var folder = DriveApp.getFolderById(folderId);
  var srcFile = DriveApp.getFileById(getSpreadsheetId_());
  var tz = "Asia/Taipei";
  try { tz = Session.getScriptTimeZone() || tz; } catch (_e0) {}
  var stamp = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), tz, "yyyy-MM-dd_HHmm");
  var name = ERP_BACKUP_NAME_PREFIX_ + stamp;

  var copy = srcFile.makeCopy(name, folder);

  // 清理舊備份：只保留最近 N 份（丟到垃圾桶，可救回）
  cleanupOldBackups_(folder, Number(ERP_BACKUP_KEEP_COPIES || 0), ERP_BACKUP_NAME_PREFIX_);

  return jsonSuccess({
    message: "Backup created",
    backup_file_id: copy.getId(),
    backup_name: name,
    keep_copies: Number(ERP_BACKUP_KEEP_COPIES || 0)
  });
}

function cleanupOldBackups_(folder, keepCopies, prefix) {
  var keep = isNaN(keepCopies) ? 0 : Math.max(0, Math.floor(keepCopies));
  var it = folder.getFiles();
  var list = [];
  while (it.hasNext()) {
    var f = it.next();
    var n = String(f.getName() || "");
    if (prefix && n.indexOf(prefix) !== 0) continue;
    list.push({ id: f.getId(), created: f.getDateCreated().getTime() });
  }
  list.sort(function (a, b) { return b.created - a.created; }); // 新→舊
  for (var i = keep; i < list.length; i++) {
    try { DriveApp.getFileById(list[i].id).setTrashed(true); } catch (_e2) {}
  }
}

/**
 * 以 session_token 續期並取回使用者資訊（重整頁面用）
 */
function sessionResume(p) {
  var tok = String(p && p.session_token || "").trim();
  if (!tok) return jsonError("session_token required");
  var o = readSessionValid_(tok);
  if (!o) return jsonError("Permission denied");
  var rid = String(o.user_id || "").trim();
  if (!isBuiltinAdmin_(rid) && !getUserRoleById_(rid)) return jsonError("Permission denied");
  var o2 = touchSession_(tok);
  if (!o2) return jsonError("Permission denied");
  var tz = "Asia/Taipei";
  try {
    tz = Session.getScriptTimeZone() || tz;
  } catch (_tz2) {}
  var expIso = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(Number(o2.exp)), tz, "yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ss");
  return jsonSuccess({
    user_id: rid,
    user_name: String(o2.user_name || ""),
    role: String(o2.role || ""),
    allowed_modules: String(o2.allowed_modules || ""),
    status: "ACTIVE",
    remember: !!o2.remember,
    session_token: tok,
    session_expires_at: expIso
  });
}

/**
 * 登出：作廢 session_token（前端仍應清除本地儲存）
 */
function sessionLogout(p) {
  var tok = String(p && p.session_token || "").trim();
  if (!tok) return jsonSuccess({ ok: true });
  deleteSession_(tok);
  return jsonSuccess({ ok: true });
}

/**
 * 不需 session_token 即可呼叫的 action
 */
var ERP_PUBLIC_ACTIONS_ = {
  login: true,
  google_login: true,
  session_resume: true,
  session_logout: true
};

/**
 * 統一驗人：除白名單外須帶有效 session_token；通過後寫入 created_by/updated_by 為 session 之 user_id（防偽造）。
 * 各 API 仍應自行 requireErpRole_（驗權）。
 * @returns {null|GoogleAppsScript.Content.TextOutput} null = 通過
 */
function assertAuthenticatedUser_(action, p) {
  var a = String(action || "").trim();
  if (ERP_PUBLIC_ACTIONS_[a]) return null;
  var tok = String(p && p.session_token || "").trim();
  if (!tok) return jsonError("session_token required");
  var sess = readSessionValid_(tok);
  if (!sess) return jsonError("Permission denied");
  var rid = String(sess.user_id || "").trim();
  // 內建 ADMIN 不在 Users Sheet，仍允許通過驗人
  if (!isBuiltinAdmin_(rid) && !getUserRoleById_(rid)) return jsonError("Permission denied");
  p.created_by = rid;
  p.updated_by = rid;
  return null;
}

/**
 * 權限檢查：依 user 表 role 判斷
 * @returns null | jsonError output
 */
function requireErpRole_(actorId, allowedRoles) {
  const id = String(actorId || "").trim();
  if (!id) return jsonError("Permission denied");
  const allow = (Array.isArray(allowedRoles) ? allowedRoles : []).map(r => String(r || "").toUpperCase());
  // 內建 ADMIN 不在 Users Sheet：僅在允許角色含 ADMIN 時放行
  if(isBuiltinAdmin_(id)){
    if (allow.indexOf("ADMIN") === -1) return jsonError("Permission denied");
    return null;
  }
  const role = getUserRoleById_(id);
  if (!role) return jsonError("Permission denied");
  if (allow.indexOf(role) === -1) return jsonError("Permission denied");
  return null;
}

// #SEC:DEV
/**
 * 開發測試用：一鍵清空「非主檔」工作表資料（保留第 1 列標題，僅清除資料列）
 * 主檔保留：product, supplier, customer, warehouse, user
 * - 僅 ADMIN 可執行（與前端：僅 ADMIN 顯示按鈕一致；避免繞過 UI 直接呼叫 action）
 */
function devClearNonMaster(p) {
  const actor = String(p && (p.updated_by || p.created_by) || "").trim();
  const perm = requireErpRole_(actor, ["ADMIN"]);
  if (perm) return perm;
  // 安全：PROD 永遠拒絕清資料
  if (getEnvName_() !== "DEV") return jsonError("Permission denied");

  const MASTER_SHEETS = ["product", "supplier", "customer", "warehouse", "user"];
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(getSpreadsheetId_());
  const toClear = Object.keys(SHEET_SCHEMAS).filter(function (name) {
    return MASTER_SHEETS.indexOf(name) === -1;
  });
  const cleared = [];
  toClear.forEach(function (name) {
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(name);
    if (!sheet) return;
    const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
    if (lastRow < 2) return;
    const lastCol = sheet.getLastColumn();
    if (lastCol < 1) return;
    sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow, lastCol).clearContent();
    cleared.push(name);
  });
  return jsonSuccess({ cleared: cleared });
}

/**
 * 冒煙測試用：建立測試資料（可重複跑）
 * - 僅 ADMIN 可執行（dev_* 預設只允許 ADMIN）
 * - 會建立：測試產品/供應商/客戶/倉庫、PO+明細、SO+明細、可出貨批次（已放行）+ 入庫異動
 * - 所有測試資料都會帶上同一個 tag，方便後續清理
 *
 * 回傳：fixtures（warehouseId/poId/soId/soItemId 等）
 */
function devSeedSmokeFixtures(p){
  const actor = String(p && (p.updated_by || p.created_by) || "").trim();
  const perm = requireErpRole_(actor, ["ADMIN"]);
  if (perm) return perm;
  // 安全：PROD 永遠拒絕灌測試資料
  if (getEnvName_() !== "DEV") return jsonError("Permission denied");

  const tag = String(p && p.tag || "").trim() || ("SMOKE-" + Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), "Asia/Taipei", "yyyyMMdd-HHmmss"));
  const now = nowIso16();

  function mkId(prefix){
    const ts = String(new Date().getTime());
    const r = String(Math.floor(Math.random()*1000)).padStart(3, "0");
    return String(prefix || "SMK") + "-" + ts + "-" + r;
  }

  const warehouseId = String((p && p.warehouse_id) || "MAIN").trim().toUpperCase();
  const productId = mkId("SMKP");
  const supplierId = mkId("SMKS");
  const customerId = mkId("SMKC");
  const poId = mkId("SMKPO");
  const poItemId = mkId("SMKPOI");
  const soId = mkId("SMKSO");
  const soItemId = mkId("SMKSOI");
  const lotId = mkId("SMKLOT");

  // 倉庫：若已存在就不重建；不存在則建立
  try{
    // 若已存在就略過；不存在才建立
    const sheet = getSheet("warehouse");
    if(sheet){
      const data = sheet.getDataRange().getDisplayValues();
      const headers = data[0] || [];
      const idxId = headers.indexOf("warehouse_id");
      let exists = false;
      if(idxId !== -1){
        for(let i=1;i<data.length;i++){
          if(String(data[i][idxId]||"").trim().toUpperCase() === warehouseId){ exists = true; break; }
        }
      }
      if(!exists){
        createGeneric("warehouse", {
          warehouse_id: warehouseId,
          warehouse_name: "SMOKE 測試倉庫",
          category: "AMBIENT",
          address: "",
          remark: "[SMOKE] " + tag,
          status: "ACTIVE",
          created_by: actor,
          created_at: now
        }, "warehouse_id");
      }
    }
  }catch(_eWh){}

  // 主檔：建立測試用產品/供應商/客戶（避免依賴既有資料）
  createGeneric("product", {
    product_id: productId,
    product_name: "SMOKE 測試產品",
    spec: "自動化測試用",
    type: "RM",
    unit: "EA",
    remark: "[SMOKE] " + tag,
    status: "ACTIVE",
    created_by: actor,
    created_at: now,
    updated_by: "",
    updated_at: ""
  }, "product_id");

  createGeneric("supplier", {
    supplier_id: supplierId,
    supplier_name: "SMOKE 測試供應商",
    contact_person: "",
    phone: "",
    email: "",
    address: "",
    country: "",
    supplier_type: "",
    supplier_flow: "",
    remark: "[SMOKE] " + tag,
    status: "ACTIVE",
    created_by: actor,
    created_at: now,
    updated_by: "",
    updated_at: ""
  }, "supplier_id");

  createGeneric("customer", {
    customer_id: customerId,
    customer_name: "SMOKE 測試客戶",
    category: "",
    contact_person: "",
    phone: "",
    email: "",
    address: "",
    country: "",
    remark: "[SMOKE] " + tag,
    status: "ACTIVE",
    created_by: actor,
    created_at: now,
    updated_by: "",
    updated_at: ""
  }, "customer_id");

  // PO + 明細：讓收貨可選
  createGeneric("purchase_order", {
    po_id: poId,
    supplier_id: supplierId,
    order_date: now.slice(0,10),
    status: "OPEN",
    remark: "[SMOKE] " + tag,
    created_by: actor,
    created_at: now,
    updated_by: "",
    updated_at: ""
  }, "po_id");

  createGeneric("purchase_order_item", {
    po_item_id: poItemId,
    po_id: poId,
    product_id: productId,
    order_qty: "10",
    received_qty: "0",
    unit: "EA",
    remark: "[SMOKE] " + tag,
    created_by: actor,
    created_at: now,
    updated_by: "",
    updated_at: ""
  }, "po_item_id");

  // SO + 明細：讓出貨可選
  createGeneric("sales_order", {
    so_id: soId,
    customer_id: customerId,
    order_date: now.slice(0,10),
    so_type: "NORMAL",
    status: "OPEN",
    remark: "[SMOKE] " + tag,
    created_by: actor,
    created_at: now,
    updated_by: "",
    updated_at: ""
  }, "so_id");

  createGeneric("sales_order_item", {
    so_item_id: soItemId,
    so_id: soId,
    product_id: productId,
    order_qty: "10",
    shipped_qty: "0",
    unit: "EA",
    remark: "[SMOKE] " + tag,
    created_by: actor,
    created_at: now,
    updated_by: "",
    updated_at: ""
  }, "so_item_id");

  // 可出貨 Lot + 入庫異動（避免還要人工 QA）
  createGeneric("lot", {
    lot_id: lotId,
    product_id: productId,
    warehouse_id: warehouseId,
    source_type: "ADJUST",
    source_id: tag,
    qty: "10",
    unit: "EA",
    type: "RM",
    manufacture_date: now.slice(0,10),
    expiry_date: "2099-12-31",
    inventory_status: "ACTIVE",
    remark: "[SMOKE] " + tag,
    status: "APPROVED",
    received_date: now.slice(0,10),
    created_by: actor,
    created_at: now,
    updated_by: "",
    updated_at: ""
  }, "lot_id");

  var _seedMv = createInventoryMovementUnlocked_({
    movement_id: mkId("SMKMV"),
    movement_type: "IN",
    lot_id: lotId,
    product_id: productId,
    warehouse_id: warehouseId,
    qty: "10",
    unit: "EA",
    ref_type: "SMOKE",
    ref_id: tag,
    issued_to: "",
    remark: "[SMOKE] seed",
    created_by: actor,
    created_at: now,
    updated_by: "",
    updated_at: "",
    system_remark: "SMOKE seed IN"
  });
  var _seedMvFail = bundleStepFail_(_seedMv);
  if (_seedMvFail) return _seedMvFail;

  return jsonSuccess({
    tag: tag,
    fixtures: {
      warehouseId: warehouseId,
      productId: productId,
      supplierId: supplierId,
      customerId: customerId,
      poId: poId,
      poItemId: poItemId,
      soId: soId,
      soItemId: soItemId,
      lotId: lotId
    }
  });
}

/**
 * 冒煙測試用：清理測試資料（可重複跑）
 * - 僅 ADMIN 可執行
 * - 以 tag 搜尋 remark 中含 [SMOKE] tag 的資料，優先走「取消/作廢」流程；主檔則標記停用即可
 */
function devCleanupSmokeFixtures(p){
  const actor = String(p && (p.updated_by || p.created_by) || "").trim();
  const perm = requireErpRole_(actor, ["ADMIN"]);
  if (perm) return perm;
  // 安全：PROD 永遠拒絕清理測試資料
  if (getEnvName_() !== "DEV") return jsonError("Permission denied");

  const tag = String(p && p.tag || "").trim();
  if(!tag) return jsonError("Missing tag");

  function findRowsByRemark_(sheetName, idField){
    const sheet = getSheet(sheetName);
    if(!sheet) return [];
    const data = sheet.getDataRange().getDisplayValues();
    if(data.length < 2) return [];
    const headers = data[0];
    const idxId = headers.indexOf(idField);
    const idxRemark = headers.indexOf("remark");
    if(idxId === -1 || idxRemark === -1) return [];
    const out = [];
    for(let i=1;i<data.length;i++){
      const rm = String(data[i][idxRemark] || "");
      if(rm.indexOf("[SMOKE] " + tag) !== -1){
        out.push(String(data[i][idxId] || "").trim());
      }
    }
    return out.filter(Boolean);
  }

  const cleaned = { cancelled: [], inactivated: [], skipped: [] };

  // 取消/作廢：採購單、銷售單（走既有 bundle）
  try{
    const poIds = findRowsByRemark_("purchase_order", "po_id");
    poIds.forEach(function(id){
      try{
        cancelPurchaseOrderBundle({ po_id: id, void_reason_code: "SMOKE", void_reason_label: "SMOKE 測試清理", void_reason_note: tag, updated_by: actor });
        cleaned.cancelled.push("PO:" + id);
      }catch(_e){ cleaned.skipped.push("PO:" + id); }
    });
  }catch(_ePo){}

  try{
    const soIds = findRowsByRemark_("sales_order", "so_id");
    soIds.forEach(function(id){
      try{
        cancelSalesOrderBundle({ so_id: id, void_reason_code: "SMOKE", void_reason_label: "SMOKE 測試清理", void_reason_note: tag, updated_by: actor });
        cleaned.cancelled.push("SO:" + id);
      }catch(_e){ cleaned.skipped.push("SO:" + id); }
    });
  }catch(_eSo){}

  // 停用主檔：產品/供應商/客戶（避免硬刪）
  function inactivateMasters_(sheet, idField){
    try{
      const sheetName = sheet;
      const ids = findRowsByRemark_(sheetName, idField);
      ids.forEach(function(id){
        try{
          updateGeneric(sheetName, { [idField]: id, status: "INACTIVE", updated_by: actor, updated_at: nowIso16() }, idField);
          cleaned.inactivated.push(sheetName + ":" + id);
        }catch(_e){ cleaned.skipped.push(sheetName + ":" + id); }
      });
    }catch(_e){}
  }
  inactivateMasters_("product", "product_id");
  inactivateMasters_("supplier", "supplier_id");
  inactivateMasters_("customer", "customer_id");

  // Lot：標記 VOID + 停用（不硬刪）
  try{
    const lotIds = findRowsByRemark_("lot", "lot_id");
    lotIds.forEach(function(id){
      try{
        updateGeneric("lot", { lot_id: id, inventory_status: "VOID", status: "INACTIVE", updated_by: actor, updated_at: nowIso16() }, "lot_id");
        cleaned.inactivated.push("lot:" + id);
      }catch(_e){ cleaned.skipped.push("lot:" + id); }
    });
  }catch(_eLot){}

  return jsonSuccess({ tag: tag, cleaned: cleaned });
}

/* =================================================
   HELPERS
================================================= */

function getSheet(name) {
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(getSpreadsheetId_());
  let sheet = ss.getSheetByName(name);

  const expected = SHEET_SCHEMAS[name];

  // 未定義 schema：維持舊行為（只回傳現有 sheet）
  if (!expected) {
    return sheet;
  }

  // 缺表：自動建立並寫入標題列
  if (!sheet) {
    sheet = ss.insertSheet(name);
    sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, expected.length).setValues([expected]);
    return sheet;
  }

  // 空表：補標題列
  const lastCol = sheet.getLastColumn();
  const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow === 0 || lastCol === 0) {
    sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, expected.length).setValues([expected]);
    return sheet;
  }

  // 標題驗證：若不一致，直接報錯，避免把資料寫錯欄位
  // 注意：Google Sheets 常見「尾端空白欄位」會讓 lastCol 變大，導致誤判 mismatch。
  // 這裡先 trim + 去掉尾端空字串，只保留有效欄位再比對。
  const current = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastCol).getValues()[0].map(function (h) {
    return String(h || "").replace(/\uFEFF/g, "").trim();
  });
  let end = current.length;
  while (end > 0 && String(current[end - 1] || "").trim() === "") end--;
  const curTrim = current.slice(0, end);

  const exp = expected.map(String);
  const sameLen = curTrim.length === exp.length;
  const sameContent = sameLen && curTrim.every((h, i) => h === exp[i]);

  if (!sameContent) {
    throw new Error(
      `Sheet header mismatch: ${name}. Please set headers exactly as: ${exp.join(", ")}`
    );
  }

  return sheet;
}

function rowToObject(headers, row) {
  const obj = {};
  headers.forEach((h, i) => obj[h] = row[i]);
  return obj;
}

/**
 * Bundle 內部：將 jsonSuccess/jsonError（TextOutput）或 plain 物件轉成可判斷的結果。
 */
function asApiResultObj_(res) {
  if (!res) return null;
  if (typeof res === "object" && res.success !== undefined) return res;
  try {
    if (typeof res.getContent === "function") {
      var txt = String(res.getContent() || "");
      if (txt) return JSON.parse(txt);
    }
  } catch (_e) {}
  return null;
}

/** Bundle 寫入步驟失敗時回傳原結果（供 bundle return）；成功則回傳 null。 */
function bundleStepFail_(res) {
  var o = asApiResultObj_(res);
  if (o && o.success === false) return res;
  return null;
}

function jsonSuccess(obj) {
  return ContentService
    .createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({ success: true, env: getEnvName_(), ...obj }))
    .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
}

function jsonError(error) {
  var errs = [].concat(error);
  var full = "";
  try{
    full = errs.map(function(x){ return String(x || ""); }).join(" ").toLowerCase();
  }catch(_e){}
  var code = "";
  try{
    if(/duplicate\s+request\s+detected/.test(full)) code = "ERR_DUPLICATE_REQUEST";
    else if(/source\s+changed/.test(full) && /reload\s+and\s+try\s+again/.test(full)) code = "ERR_SOURCE_CHANGED";
    else if(/already\s+(posted|cancelled|canceled)/.test(full)) code = "ERR_ALREADY_PROCESSED";
    else if(/unknown\s+or\s+missing\s+action/.test(full)) code = "ERR_UNKNOWN_ACTION";
    else if(/permission\s+denied/.test(full)) code = "ERR_PERMISSION_DENIED";
    else if(/session_token\s+required/.test(full)) code = "ERR_SESSION_REQUIRED";
  }catch(_e2){}
  return ContentService
    .createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({
      success: false,
      env: getEnvName_(),
      error_code: code,
      errors: errs
    }))
    .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
}

function jsonErrorCode(code, error) {
  return ContentService
    .createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({
      success: false,
      env: getEnvName_(),
      error_code: String(code || "").trim().toUpperCase(),
      errors: [].concat(error)
    }))
    .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
}
